问题：U1700 的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络？
参考答案：U1700 是 LP87702-Q1；VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 接 VCC3V3，FB_B0 接 VCC1V1，FB_B1 接 VCC1V8，VOUT_BST 接 VCC50，EN1 接 MCU_5V_EN。
实际回答：根据现有证据，无法完整回答 U1700 的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络。以下按子问题说明证据覆盖情况、缺失信息及证据冲突。 --- ### 子问题 1：U1700 的关键引脚列表有哪些？ **状态：部分覆盖（partial）** **已获取的证据：** - 电路设计证据 `circuit:21:instance:U1700` 确认了 U1700 是一个实例，但未提供任何引脚名称或编号的具体内容。 - 文档文本证据 `document:2d268c72a4fc40c1`（出自 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`，Sheet 名称列表）未包含 U1700 的引脚定义。 - 文档文本证据 `document:72947b599dcff97a`（出自同一文档，表格内容）列出了 MCU 引脚（如 P10.6、P14.4、ESR1、ESR0 等），但未明确这些引脚属于 U1700，且该表格描述的是 MCU 组件（F01/MCU），而非 U1700 器件。 - 数据手册证据 `document:eeb35b1228d7836f`（`FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`）提供了 **TPS6287x-Q1** 系列降压转换器的引脚功能表（引脚包括 COMP、GOSNS、VOSNS、EN、VIN、GND、SW、PG、MODE/SYNC、SDA、SCL、SYNC_OUT、VSEL、FSEL 等），但未明确该器件与 U1700 的对应关系。 **缺失信息：** - 缺少 U1700 对应的具体器件型号或数据手册，无法确认其关键引脚列表。 - 缺少直接描述 U1700 引脚分配的任何文档文本或电路原理图细节。 **结论：** 无法确定 U1700 的完整关键引脚列表。 --- ### 子问题 2：每个关键引脚在原理图中连接的网络是什么？ **状态：声称覆盖（covered），但实际证据不足** **已获取的证据：** - 电路设计证据（如 `circuit:21:module_connection:Group 14 · Ethernet/PHY:...`）仅涉及以太网/PHY 模块的网络连接（如 B_D_ETH_1000_N、B_D_ETH_1000_P、B_D_ETH_100_N、B_D_ETH_100_P、ETH_INH、GND），但未提及 U1700 的任何引脚。 - 文档文本证据 `document:72947b599dcff97a` 中列出了 MCU 引脚的网络描述（如 P10.6 连接 PMS_HWCFG5IN、P14.4 连接 PMS_HWCFG6IN、ESR1 连接 NMI、ESR0 连接 WARMRESET），但未明确这些引脚属于 U1700，且该表格描述的是 MCU 组件，而非 U1700。 - 其他文档文本证据（如 `document:2d268c72a4fc40c1`、`document:5e69a562758e3536`、`document:a4da2fc271443e28`、`document:317116411fd54deb`、`document:0eb6659a764f4d29`）均来自 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`，内容为 Sheet 名称列表或配置表格，未包含 U1700 的引脚网络连接。 **缺失信息：** - 缺少 U1700 在原理图中实际连接的网络名称。 - 缺少任何将 U1700 引脚与具体网络相关联的证据。 **结论：** 无法确定 U1700 任何引脚所连接的网络。 --- ### 证据冲突说明 根据 `coverage_matrix.conflicts`，存在以下冲突（均来自 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 和 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`）： 1. **字段 `unnamed`**：同一文档中出现了取值 0、1、2、3、4、5、6，但无上下文说明这些值对应哪个器件或引脚。 2. **字段 `note`**：在 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` 中，同一字段既有 “page” 又有 “qualified v”，含义不明。 3. **字段 `none`**：大量取值（如 `1`、`a18`、`a4`、`b`、`b15`、`config`、`eq6l`、`gpio`、`hi-z`、`nmi`、`p10.6`、`p14.4`、`p15.1`、`p23.0`、`p23.1`、`pms_hwcfg5in`、`pms_hwcfg6in`、`u19`、`v20`、`warmreset` 等）彼此矛盾，且未明确关联 U1700。 4. **字段 `gpioxx gpioyy`**：同一文档中出现了 `00`、`01`、`10`、`11` 四种取值，无进一步解释。 这些冲突表明文档内容混乱，无法从中提取关于 U1700 的一致信息。 --- ### 总结 **缺失项：** - U1700 的器件型号或数据手册。 - U1700 的关键引脚列表（引脚名称、编号、类型）。 - U1700 各引脚在原理图中连接的网络名称。 **建议：** 需要提供 U1700 对应的器件数据手册（如 LP87702-Q1 或 TPS6287x-Q1 等）以及包含 U1700 引脚的原理图详细数据，才能回答此问题。

<table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F14</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的方案TPS62872。 VCC3V3 to VCCOV75 is applied to the kernel power supply of EQ6, 问题：网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片？
参考答案：VCC3V3 有 52 个连接；主要 IC 至少包括 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600。
实际回答：根据所提供的证据，网络 VCC3V3 上连接了多个主要芯片，同时其衍生子网络也连接了若干其他芯片。以下基于证据详细列出。 ### 网络 VCC3V3 上连接的主要芯片 基于电路设计证据（来源：825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF），网络 `VCC3V3` 直接连接了以下芯片： - **U1500**（引脚 VIN） - **U1600**（引脚 VCCIN） - **U1700**（引脚 VIN_B0_1, VIN_B1_1, VANA, VIN_B0_2, VIN_B1_2） - **U1800**（引脚 VIN, VIN, MODE/SYNC） - **U1802**（引脚 VIN） - **U1804**（引脚 VIN, VIN） ### 网络 VCC3V3 衍生子网络上连接的主要芯片 网络 `VCC3V3` 衍生出多个子网络，这些子网络也连接了其他主要芯片，具体如下： - **VCC3V3_CAM_ACQ**：连接 **U900A**（引脚 AN12/EVADC_G1CH4/EDSADC_EDS0PB）。 - **VCC3V3_CAM_FB**：此网络未连接芯片，仅连接了电阻、连接器等无源器件。 - **VCC3V3_EQ**：连接 **U1802**（引脚 QOD, VOUT）、**U400-2**（引脚 VDD_RING_8_0-1, VDD_RING_8_0-2, VDD_RING_8_0-3, VDD_RING_9_0）以及 **Y500**（引脚 VDD，Y500 为振荡器/晶振器件，可视为小型芯片）。 - **VCC3V3_ETH**：连接 **U1500**（引脚 QOD, VOUT）和 **U1501**（引脚 EN, IN）。 - **VCC3V3_MCU**：连接 **U1200**（引脚 VI/O）、**U1203**（引脚 VI/O）、**U1204**（引脚 VI/O）、**U1205**（引脚 VI/O）、**U1302**（引脚 VI/O）、**U1303**（引脚 VI/O）、**U1304**（引脚 VI/O）、**U1305**（引脚 VI/O）、**U503**（引脚 VCCB）、**U900C**（引脚 VEVRSB, VEXT, VEXT, VEXT, VEXT, VEXT, VFLEX）以及 **Y1100**（引脚 std-by, VCC，Y1100 为振荡器/晶振器件）。 - **VCC3V3_SW**：连接 **U901**（引脚 S2）。 ### 缺失信息 证据中未提供这些芯片的具体型号或功能描述，因此无法进一步确认每颗芯片的详细用途（如电源管理、微控制器、接口芯片等）。若需此类信息，需查阅相关芯片的数据手册。

问题：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？它输出哪些电压，软件如何配置？
参考答案：主 PMIC 是 U1700，即 LP87702-Q1，输入 VCC3V3，输出包括 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 和 VCC50；软件配置依据 HSI 7.2 节 LP87702 配置说明，通过 I²C 相关信号配置。
实际回答：好的，根据您提供的证据，现对您的问题进行回答。 ### 1. 主PMIC是哪颗芯片？ 给板子供电的主PMIC是 **LP87702**。 * **来源说明**：多个证据均明确指出此方案。例如，证据[9]（文档ID: b6943ae371d76077）在“模块方案”小节中说明：“VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50...现选择LP87702 PMIC方案”。证据[19]（文档ID: 5e69a562758e3536）也明确指出：“VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。” 电路连接证据[1]（电路ID: 825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2，实体ID: MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1）中，U1700即为LP87702芯片。 ### 2. 它输出哪些电压？ LP87702将输入的VCC3V3转换为以下电压输出： * **VCC1V8** * **VCC1V1** * **VCC50** * **来源说明**：证据[9]和[19]（文档ID: b6943ae371d76077, 5e69a562758e3536）均明确描述了LP87702的输出电压轨。证据[15]（文档ID: ed178b5d659e251d）列出的电源轨阈值表中，包含了与LP87702输出相关的`VCC1V8`、`VCC1V1`和`VCC50`。 ### 3. 软件如何配置？ 软件主要通过 **I2C通信** 对LP87702进行配置。具体配置方式如下： 1. **通信接口**：使用MCU的`MCU_SDA_P15.5`（I2C数据线）和`MCU_SCL_P15.4`（I2C时钟线）引脚进行I2C通信。LP87702的I2C设备地址为 **0x08**。 * **来源说明**：证据[9]（文档ID: b6943ae371d76077）中提到“用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能”。证据[20]（文档ID: 0a2d86c848720c0c）明确记载了I2C信号和地址：“外挂LP87702和TPS62872 LP87702地址：0x08”。 2. **配置内容**： * **使能输出**：通过MCU的`P15.3`引脚用于使能LP87702的5V输出（VCC50）。 * **设置输出电压**：通过I2C通信设置VCC1V8和VCC1V1的输出电压并使能。 * **配置过/欠压阈值**：软件需要根据WCCA（最坏情况分析）计算结果设置过压和欠压监控阈值。证据[15]（文档ID: ed178b5d659e251d）提供了需要设置的电压阈值表，例如`VCC1V8`的电压范围需在`1.613V`（Min）到`2.012V`（Max）之间。 * **配置寄存器**：LP87702运行期间，MCU需要通过I2C配置其寄存器。证据[12]（文档ID: d69d04d302326d46）中明确指出：“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”。证据[16]（文档ID: 0987620a09f7a3bf）则提供了寄存器配置的示例，如`FS_I_OVUV_SAFE_REACTION1`寄存器，用于配置LDO1过压（OV）或欠压（UV）时的反应（如是否触发复位）。 * **来源说明**：综合证据[9]、[12]、[15]、[16]、[19]和[20]。 ### 缺失信息说明 * **详细的软件配置寄存器列表**：虽然证据[12]提到了“Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”，但提供的证据片段中并未包含该表格的完整内容。因此，**缺失LP87702需要配置的寄存器的完整列表、具体寄存器地址、位域定义以及各寄存器在上电后的初始化值**。 * **电压输出序列**：尽管文档提到了上下电时序，但提供的证据中**未明确说明VCC1V8、VCC1V1和VCC50这三个电压轨的上电和下电顺序**。 ### 证据冲突说明 根据您提供的“冲突”信息，部分证据在描述其他字段时存在不一致，但**这些冲突值与回答您关于主PMIC（LP87702）的识别、输出电压及软件配置的核心问题不直接相关**。例如，冲突信息中关于“tcan1044a”和“tcan1145”的冲突，以及“register”、“symbol”等字段的冲突，均来源于对CAN收发器或寄存器描述的不同部分，不涉及LP87702本身。因此，这些冲突不影响上述结论的准确性。

KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F05</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>Camera实现图像采集功能，根据ADAS系统需求，选择图像传感器芯片IMX-728作为camera主芯片。 The Camera realizes image acquisition function. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. SM TSR：The ECU shall integrate the processing platform safety mechanism for SPF._x000D_ When the processor SPF is confirmed, the ECU shall:_x000D_ 1. stop outputing ADAS request and;_x000D_ 2. disable the CAN communication and;_x000D_ 3. reset processing platform._x000D_ FHTI < 300 ms._x000D_ When the processor SPF is no longer detected after the processor reset, the ECU shall clear the processor SPF. ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_006; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：软件应该避免PLFASH中出现全0或者全1。如果可能出现全0情况，软件应该使用0x36填充，不能写全1填充。 ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_021; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：软件应保证受CPU0 ENDINIT保护的寄存器被写操作时，正确解开CPU ENDINIT保护，写完寄存器后，再次锁上CPU ENDINIT保护。 解开CPU ENDINIT保护前，软件应屏蔽中断，防止写寄存器时间超时。 解开CPU ENDINIT保护后，写寄存器的时间小于1us ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_016; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：After SMU reset, the SW shall read the SMU fault status register and store SMU fault DTC and Snapshot in NVM. The SW can clear SMU fault DTC and Snapshot by the diagnostic sevice. （备注：恒润下线时清除Snapshot信息） The SW can clear SMU fault DTC and Snapshot if no SMU fault for consecutive TBD ignition cycle . ——SYS SM | READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. technical safety concept.适用范围 Range of Application此文档适用于奇瑞EQ6L T1G ADAS项目的功能安全开发全过程。This document is applied for the whole functional safety development process of EQ6L T1G ADAS project.读者对象 问题：0.75V 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOSNS 对应 VCC0V75；配置要求见 HSI 7.3 节 TPS62872 配置。
实际回答：根据检索到的文档，0.75V电源的相关信息如下： ### 1. 0.75V电源由哪颗芯片产生？ **芯片型号：** **TPS62872**（德州仪器 TI 方案） **来源：** * 文档[1]（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中“模块方案”部分明确说明：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电...现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872”。 * 文档[4]（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中“模块功能”部分再次确认：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电...现选型TI的方案TPS62872”。 * 文档[6]（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中表格第2行也列出了相同的芯片信息：“F14...TPS62872，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC0V75电压输出”。 ### 2. 该芯片的输入是什么？ **输入电压：** **VCC3V3（3.3V）** **来源：** * 文档[1]、[4]、[6]、[8]和[16]中均指出，TPS62872的输入为“VCC3V3”，即3.3V电源。 ### 3. 文档中对该0.75V电源有哪些配置要求？ 文档中提到的配置要求如下： **3.1. 使能控制** * **控制方式：** 由MCU的GPIO引脚 **P22.0** 进行使能控制。 * **控制信号：** `MCU_VCC0V75_EN`。 * **逻辑：** 高电平使能VCC0V75电源，低电平关断。 * **来源：** 文档[1]（“用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片”）、文档[8]（HW Interface Component表格中明确列出了P22.0引脚及使能控制信号）。 **3.2. 输出电压设置** * **设置方式：** 通过 **I2C通信** 设置输出电压。 * **通信引脚：** MCU引脚 `MCU_SDA_P15.5` 和 `MCU_SCL_P15.4`。 * **来源：** 文档[1]（“用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压”）。 **3.3. 上电时序要求** * **功能：** VCC0V75是EQ6L系统所需的6路电源之一，用于给内核供电。 * **时序控制：** 由MCU按照时序控制其上电。 * **来源：** 文档[5]（“MCU按照时序控制EQ6的电源...VCC0V75...上电”）。 **3.4. 电压精度要求** * **精度要求：** 0.75V ± 3% （或 0.75V ± 3.3%） * **来源：** 文档[3]（“精度要求：0V75±3%”和“精度要求：0.75V±3.3%”）。 **3.5. 电压采样要求** * **采样方式：** MCU通过ADC引脚 **AN19** 对VCC0V75进行电压采样。 * **采样信号：** `0V75_ACQ`。 * **硬件滤波：** 硬件滤波截止频率 ≥ 121.7Hz，硬件滤波时间 ≤ 1.6ms。 * **来源：** 文档[3]（“MCU采样EQ6l系统的VCC0V75电源电压...硬件滤波截止频率：≥121.7Hz...硬件滤波时间：≤1.6ms”）。 ### 缺失信息 文档中明确说明TPS62872通过I2C通信设置输出电压，但**未提供在I2C寄存器中具体配置输出电压为0.75V时的寄存器地址、默认值及配置步骤**。详细的配置方法需参考TPS62872的数据手册（datasheet）。

MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Tl's customers are responsible for determining suitability of components for their purposes, as well as validating and testing their design implementation to confirm systemfunctionality. 10.1 Application Information The following section discusses selection of the external components to complete the power supply design for typical a application. 10.2 Typical Application 10.2.1 Design Requirements Preliminary Calculations The maximum allowable deviation of the power supply is ±3.3%. The DC accuracy of the TPS6287x-Q1 is specified as ±1%, therefore, the maximum output voltage variation during a transient is given by: VoUT = ± VouT × (3.3% - 1%) = ±17.25 mV 10.2.2 Detailed Design Procedure The following subsections describe how to calculate the external components required to meet the specified transient requirements of a given application. The calculations include the worst-case variation of components and use the RMS method to combine the variation of uncorrelated parameters. 10.2.2.1 Selecting the Inductor The TPS6287x-Q1 devices have been optimized for inductors in the range 50 nH to 300 nH. If the transient response of the converter is limited by the slew rate of the current in the inductor, using a smaller inductor can improve performance. However, the output ripple current increases as the value of the inductor decreases and higher output current ripple generates higher output voltage ripple, which adds to the transient overshoot or undershoot. The optimal configuration for a given application is always a trade-off between a number of parameters. Tl recommends a starting value of 110 nH for typical applications. | HW Interface Component ：B; None：F32/POWER; None：1V25用于MCU核供电，精度：±10% 由MCU的EVRC进行控制; MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：Y17; None：P32.0; None：P32.0/GTM_TIM3_IN2_5/GTM_TIM2_IN2_5/GTM_TOUT36/VGATE1N; None：GPIO; None：——; None：/; None：O; None：MCU1.25V电源驱动信号; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：W17; None：P32.1; None：P32.1/GTM_TIM3_IN3_15/GTM_TOUT37/VGATE1P; None：GPIO; None：——; None：/; None：O; None：MCU1.25V电源驱动信号; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：P8; None：VDD; None：VDD; None：POWER; None：VCC1V25_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：1V25供电，MCU核电源域; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：P13; None：VDD; None：VDD; None：POWER; None：VCC1V25_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：1V25供电，MCU核电源域; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：N7; None：VDD; None：VDD; None：POWER; None：VCC1V25_MCU; None：/; None：/; None：1V25供电，MCU核电源域; None：/; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | During start-up, the output voltage ramps up to the target value set by the VSEL pin before ramping up or down to any new value programmed to the device over the I2C interface. 9.3.6.3Non-DefaultOutputVoltageSetpoint If none of the default voltage range or voltage setpoint combinations are suitable for the application, the user can change these device settings through I2C before the user enables the device. Then, when the user pulls the EN pin high, the device starts up with the desired start-up voltage. Note that if the user changes the device settings through I?C while the device is ramping, the device ignores the changes until the ramp is complete 9.3.6.4 Dynamic Voltage Scaling If the user changes the output voltage setpoint while the DC/DC converter is operating, the device ramps up or down to the new voltage setting in a controlled way. The VRAMP[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register sets the slew rate when the device ramps from one voltage to another during DVS (see Table 9-6). Note that ramping the output to a higher voltage requires additional output current, so that during DvS, the converter must generate a total output current given by: dVour lour = lour c) + Cour dVOouT where · lout is the total current the converter must generate while ramping to a higher voltage. louT(Dc) is the DC load current.· Cout is the total output capacitance. dVout/dt is the slew rate of the output voltage (programmable in the range 0.5 mV/μs to 10 mV/us). For correct operation, ensure that the total output current during DVS does not exceed the current limit of the device. 9.3.7 Compensation (CoMP) The CoMP pin is the connection point for an external compensation network. A series-connected resistor and capacitor to GOSNS is sufficient for typical applications. The series-connected resistor also provides enough scope to optimize the loop response for a wide range of operating conditions. When using multiple devices in a stacked configuration, all devices share a common compensation network, and the COMP pin makes sure there is equal current sharing between them (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.8 ModeSelection and Clock Synchronization (MODE/SYNC) A high level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects forced PWM operation. A low level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects power save operation, in which, the device automatically transitions between PWM and PFM, according to the load conditions. | None：MCU外挂20MHz晶体; MCU IPC-7525 may have alternate6. design recommendations. | 10.4PowerSupplyRecommendations The TPS6287x-Q1 family has no special requirements for its input power supply. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | td(RAMP) = 500 μs td(RAMP) = 1 ms (default) td(RAMP) = 2 ms td(RAMP) = 4 ms· The device ignores new values until the soft-start sequence is complete if the user programs the following when the device soft-start sequence has already started: A new output voltage setpoint (VOuT[7:o]) An output voltage range (VRANGE[1:0]) Soft-start time (SSTIME[1:o]) settings If the user change the value of VSET[7:0] during soft start, the device first ramps to the value that VSET[7:0] had when the soft-start sequence began. Then, when soft start is complete, the device ramps up or down to the new value. The device can start up into a prebiased output. In this case, only a portion of the internal voltage ramp is seen externally (see Figure 9-9). Note that the device always operates in DcM during the start-up ramp, regardless of other configuration settings or operating conditions. 9.3.5SwitchingFrequencySelection During device initialization, a resistor-to-digital converter in the device determines the state of the FSEL pin and sets the switching frequency of the DC/DC converter according to Table 9-2. For a reliable voltage setting, ensure there is no stray current path connected to the FSEL pin and that the1. parasitic capacitance between the FSEL pin and GND is less than 1o0 pF. identical circuit detects the state of the VSEL pin - see Section 9.3.6.2). Detection of the state of the FSEL pin works as follows: To detect the most significant bit (MSB), the circuit opens S1 and S2, and the input buffer detects if a high or a low level is connected to the FSEL pin. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 To detect the least significant bit (LSB): If the MSB was 0, the circuit closes S1. If the input buffer detects a high level, LSB = 1. If the circuit detects a low level, LSB = 0. If the MSB was 1, the circuit closes S2. If the input buffer detects a low level, LSB = 0. If the circuit detects a· | 9.4 Device Functional Modes 9.4.1 Power-On Reset The device operates in POR mode when the supply voltage is less than the POR threshold, 1.4 V (typical). In POR mode, no functions are available and the content of the device registers is not valid. The device leaves POR mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage increases above the POR threshold 9.4.2 Undervoltage Lockout The device operates in UVLO mode when the supply voltage is between the POR and UVLO thresholds. If the device enters UVLO mode from POR mode, no functions are available. If the device enters UVLO mode from standby mode, the output discharge function is available. The content of the device registers is valid in UVLO mode. The device leaves UVLO mode and enters POR mode when the supply voltage decreases below the POR threshold. The device leaves UVLO mode and enters standby mode when the supply voltage increases above the UVLO threshold. 9.4.3 Standby The device operates in standby mode when the supply voltage is greater than the UVLO threshold (and the device has completed its initialization) and any of the following conditions is true: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. SWEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register.· The device junction temperature is greater than the thermal shutdown threshold. ·The supply voltage is greater than the OVLO threshold. The device initializes for 4o0 μs (typical) after the supply voltage increases above the UVLO threshold voltage following a device power-on reset. If the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold but not below the POR threshold, the device does not reinitialize when the supply voltage increases again. During initialization, the device reads the state of the FSEL, VSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. The following functions are available in standby mode: · I2C interface ·Output discharge ·Power good Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The device leaves standby mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold. The device leaves standby mode and enters on mode when all of the following conditions are true: · A high-level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The device junction temperature is below the thermal shutdown threshold.· · The supply voltage is below the OVLO threshold. | <table><caption> Table9-5.DefaultOutputVoltageSetpoints</caption> <tr><td 问题：3.3V 电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？
参考答案：HSI 2.3.12 节声明存在 3V3 过压保护电路；EDF 只能用于说明观察到的二极管或 TVS 连接拓扑，不能在没有数据手册证据时断言具体阈值或保护能力。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，3.3V电源存在过压保护，其实现方式并非在3.3V输出端，而是在其输入端（12V电池电压）进行保护。具体如下： ### 1. 3.3V电源的电路设计文档中是否包含过压保护电路？ **结论：** 是的，包含。 **来源说明：** * 证据[1] (600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx) 在模块F11（KL30电源管理电路）中提到：“后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护”。这里的输入是KL30（12V电池电压），而3.3V电源由该输入转换而来。 * 证据[10] (600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx) 在模块F11/F12...的电源方案中也明确提到：“后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护”。 * 证据[13] (600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx) 的目录中明确列出了“3V3过压保护电路”这一章节。 ### 2. 如果存在过压保护，具体是如何实现的？ **结论：** 过压保护通过在12V电源输入（KL30/UBD）端增加双向TVS管来实现，用于抑制瞬态过压。此外，用于产生3.3V的电源转换芯片（TPS62872）自身也集成了过压锁定（OVLO）功能。 **来源说明：** * **输入端TVS管保护：** * 证据[1] (600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx) 描述：“后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护”。 * 证据[10] (600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx) 描述：“硬件电路设计...在UBD网络...后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护”。 * **电源芯片的过压锁定（OVLO）功能：** * 证据[9] (FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf) 是芯片TPS62872的文档，其中提到：“The device has an overvoltage lockout function... The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical).”。该芯片用于将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75，其OVLO功能保护的是其自身的输入电压（VCC3V3），当输入电压高于6.3V时，芯片会停止工作。 * 证据[2] (FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf) 是芯片TPS6287x-Q1系列的文档，其中也提到了“OVLO”功能。 ### 3. 缺失信息 * 证据中提到了“3V3过压保护电路”这一章节（证据[13]），但提供的证据片段中并未包含该章节的具体电路设计细节（如具体使用的TVS管型号、保护阈值等）。因此，**关于3.3V过压保护电路的具体实现细节（例如，是否在3.3V输出端也设置了独立的TVS管或过压保护IC）未在提供的证据中明确。** * **证据冲突：** * 证据[3]中，关于MCU电源VCC1V25_MCU的精度描述，同时出现了“1.2-1.35v”和“3.3v”两个值。其中“3.3V”是VCC3V3_MCU的电压值，而“1.2-1.35V”是VCC1V25_MCU的精度范围。这两个值属于不同电源轨的描述，并非对同一对象的冲突。 * 证据[2]中，关于“ob”字段，同时出现了“en”和“pg”两个值。这可能是文档中不同表格或不同功能的描述，例如“en”可能指使能（Enable），“pg”可能指电源正常（Power-Good）。由于缺乏上下文，无法确定其具体含义或判断是否为冲突，**此处列出该冲突，但无法得出确定结论。**

When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. Page Changes from Revision A (July 2022) to Revision B (January 2023) | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><caption> Table 9-16. The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. 问题：板上用 TPS22918 做了哪些电源开关？逐个给出输入、输出和使能。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8 到 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：好的，根据您提供的证据，我对板上使用的 TPS22918 电源开关进行了梳理。以下是基于证据的详细回答。 ### 板上 TPS22918 电源开关使用情况 板上共使用了 **3** 个 TPS22918 负载开关芯片，其参考编号分别为 **U1500**、**U1802** 和 **U1803**。 以下是每个芯片的输入、输出和使能引脚的详细信息： #### 1. 芯片 U1500 (对应文档中的 F17 模块) * **功能描述**: 将 VCC3V3 系统电源转换为 VCC3V3_ETH，用于以太网 (ETH) 供电 [14]。 * **输入引脚 (VIN)**: 连接至 **VCC3V3** 系统电源 [14]。 * **输出引脚 (VOUT)**: 连接至 **VCC3V3_ETH** 电源网络 [14]。 * **使能引脚 (EN)**: 连接至 **MCU_3V3_ETH_EN** 信号，该信号由 MCU 的 **P21.0** 引脚控制 [8][14][18]。 #### 2. 芯片 U1802 (对应文档中的 F16 模块) * **功能描述**: 将 VCC3V3 系统电源转换为 VCC3V3_EQ，用于 EQ6 芯片的 IO 和摄像头供电 [13]。 * **输入引脚 (VIN)**: 连接至 **VCC3V3** 系统电源 [13]。 * **输出引脚 (VOUT)**: 连接至 **VCC3V3_EQ** 电源网络 [13]。 * **使能引脚 (EN)**: 连接至 **MCU_3V3_EQ_EN** 信号，该信号由 MCU 的 **P20.3** 引脚控制 [7][13][18]。 #### 3. 芯片 U1803 (对应文档中的 F19 模块) * **功能描述**: 将 VCC1V8 电源转换为 VCC1V8_EQ，用于 EQ6 芯片的供电 [12]。 * **输入引脚 (VIN)**: 连接至 **VCC1V8** 电源 [12]。 * **输出引脚 (VOUT)**: 连接至 **VCC1V8_EQ** 电源网络 [12]。 * **使能引脚 (EN)**: 连接至 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN** 信号，该信号由 MCU 的 **P20.0** 引脚控制 [6][12][15]。 --- ### 缺失信息与证据冲突说明 1. **缺失信息 (Gap)**: * **引脚连接细节**: 尽管文档描述了每个芯片的功能、输入和输出电源，但提供的电路图证据中并未直接给出 U1500、U1802、U1803 这三个芯片的 **VIN (输入引脚)** 和 **VOUT (输出引脚)** 所连接的具体网络名称。以上关于输入、输出的信息是基于文档描述推断得出的。 * **部分使能信号**: 对于芯片 U1500，证据 [8] 明确指出其使能引脚 (ON) 连接至 `MCU_3V3_ETH_EN` 网络，并由 MCU 的 P21.0 引脚控制。但对于芯片 U1802 和 U1803，虽然在文档 [7] 和 [6] 中找到了其使能引脚连接的网络 `MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` 和 `MCU_1V8_EQ_EN`，但未找到直接证据表明这些网络直接连接到 MCU 的对应引脚。这些连接关系是根据文档 [13] 和 [12] 的模块方案描述推断的。 2. **证据冲突 (Conflicts)**: * 在提供的证据质量评分中，对于多个字段（如“fsw”、“lol”等）存在不同来源的取值冲突。这些冲突主要来源于芯片数据手册 (datasheet) `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` 中的不同参数。由于您的问题不涉及这些具体参数，**此冲突不影响当前答案的正确性**。在回答关于电源开关功能、输入、输出和使能的问题时，所有证据指向一致，无冲突。

The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>模块序号 Module number</td><td>模块名称 Module name</td><td>功能定义 Function 问题：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？
参考答案：供电链路为 VCC3V3 → U1500（TPS22918，ON=MCU_3V3_ETH_EN）→ VCC3V3_ETH → U1501（TPS74501 LDO）→ VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：好的，基于提供的证据，我对以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源供电链路的分析如下。请注意，所有信息均严格来源于所给证据，对于缺失的信息，我会明确指出。 ### 以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源供电链路分析 #### 子问题1：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源轨在原理图中对应的网络名称是什么？该电源轨的电压、电流规格在芯片手册中如何定义？ **回答：** * **网络名称：** 在原理图中，以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源轨对应网络名称为 **`VCC1V0_ETH`**。证据[6]显示该网络连接了多个元件，包括电感 `L1403`、电容 `C1420`、`C1506`、`C1507` 以及电源转换器件 `U1501` 的输出端，表明了其为电源网络。 * **电压/电流规格：** **证据中未找到**。虽然在文档[18]中提到了该模块（F24，ETH）基于 `DP83TC812R` 的 100BASE-T1 通讯，但提供的证据（包括针对芯片手册 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` 的检索）中，**并未包含该芯片关于 1.0V 电源轨的电压、电流规格定义**。这是当前信息中的一个缺口。 #### 子问题2：该1.0V电源轨的供电来源是哪个电源转换器件（如LDO、DC-DC）？其输入电压和输出电流能力如何？ **回答：** * **供电来源：** 证据[6]明确指出，`VCC1V0_ETH` 网络连接到了器件 **`U1501`** 的输出端 (`U1501.OUT`)。因此，`U1501` 就是为以太网 PHY 提供 1.0V 电源的转换器件。 * **器件型号与参数：** **证据中未找到**。虽然证据[6]标识了 `U1501` 是电源输出端，并且证据[5]显示 `&1V0_ETH_ACQ` 是一个用于 ADC 采样的信号，但所有证据中**均未明确 `U1501` 的具体型号（如 LDO 或 DC-DC 的具体型号）、其输入电压以及输出电流能力**。这是当前信息中的一个缺口。 #### 子问题3：上一级电源转换器件的输入电源从何而来？是否存在中间滤波、保护电路或电源序列？ **回答：** * **上一级电源：** 证据[18]中的表格显示，存在一个名为 `TPS22918` 的负载开关（模块F17），其功能是“VCC3V3输入，VCC3V3_ETH输出”。这表明 `VCC3V3_ETH` 是仅用于以太网模块的一个电源。然而，**证据中未明确说明 `VCC3V3_ETH` 是否直接作为 `U1501` 的输入电源**。`VCC1V0_ETH` 的上一级电源来源尚不明确。 * **中间滤波、保护电路或电源序列：** * **滤波电路：** 证据[6]显示，在 `VCC1V0_ETH` 网络上连接了电容 `C1420`、`C1506`、`C1507` 和电感 `L1403`。这些元件通常在电源输出端起到滤波作用。 * **保护电路与电源序列：** **证据中未找到**。在提供的文档和原理图信息中，**没有描述针对 `VCC1V0_ETH` 或 `VCC3V3_ETH` 的过压、过流保护电路，也没有描述相关的电源上电/断电时序要求或实现电路**。这是当前信息中的一个缺口。 ### 证据冲突 在提供的证据中，存在一些冲突，但**这些冲突与直接回答“以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？”这个问题并不直接相关**。这些冲突主要集中在文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`中，内容涉及模块功能安全需求、电源监控、故障模式等不同字段的取值差异。例如，对于一个功能安全需求，该文档中既有“monitor”又有“sbc”的描述。这些冲突不影响本问题的核心答案，但表明该文档内部存在信息不一致的情况。 ### 总结与缺失信息 | 电源层级 | 网络/器件 | 是否确认 | 信息来源 | 缺失信息 | | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | | 以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源 | 网络 `VCC1V0_ETH` | 已确认 | 证据[6] | 电压、电流规格（来自芯片手册） | | 1.0V 电源转换器件 | 器件 `U1501` | 已确认 | 证据[6] | 具体型号、输入电压、输出电流能力 | | 上级电源输入 | 未知 | 未确认 | 无 | `U1501` 的输入电源网络名称及来源 | | 滤波/保护/时序 | 部分滤波（`L1403`等） | 部分确认 | 证据[6] | 完整的保护电路设计和电源时序要求 | **结论：** 目前证据仅能确认以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源网络名为 `VCC1V0_ETH`，并由一个标记为 `U1501` 的器件供电。该电源输出端有基本滤波电路。但关于 `U1501` 的具体参数、其上级电源的来源以及完整的电源链路（包括保护、时序等）信息，在当前提供的证据中均缺失。

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table>
